Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the geology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 930 and the convergence rate is O(575/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by geology generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 930, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 575. QED.
